Title: Ausbund, das ist: etliche schoene christliche Lieder
Creator: Anabaptist community (collective/anonymous hymnal)
Publisher: Lancaster, PA: Johann Baer, 1846
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: Published 1846; public domain by date. Archive.org
  item metadata carries no explicit possible-copyright-status tag but
  shows access-restricted-item: None (freely downloadable, digitized
  by Princeton Theological Seminary in the open 'americana'
  collection), checked directly against the item's own metadata,
  2026-09-25, this session; pd-us-by-date applies independently
  given the 1846 publication date.
Language: deu
Source: Internet Archive item ausbunddasistetl00lanc
  (archive.org/details/ausbunddasistetl00lanc), fetched directly
  (curl, raw bytes) from the item's own _djvu.txt full-text file,
  2026-09-25, this session.
Provenance note: Body text unaltered including its own OCR errors
  (German). The Anabaptist movement's own hymnal, still in liturgical
  use among the Amish. This volume is physically bound with three
  further texts per its own title page (a Confessio oder
  Bekanntnitz, a historical report, and six more spiritual songs) -
  not individually identified or assessed this pass. Per this
  project's own INTAKE.md rule, this German text is a second
  witness, never primary evidence for a Representative.
